# Semiconductor Internships & New-Grad Jobs

A daily-updated list of semiconductor internships and new-grad roles for [Olin EDGE Consortium](https://app.notion.com/p/EDGE-Consortium-Olin-40006a00232f80b69684dc0de1c93965) scholars, part of the national [EDGE Consortium](https://www.edgeconsortium.org/).

### 🔎 [Search, filter and track your applications on the website →](https://rcyaon.github.io/college-semiconductor-jobs/)

The website also has an application tracker (Saved → Applied → Interviewing → Offer). It saves in your browser, and you can optionally sync it to your GitHub account to use it on any device.

<!-- JOBS:START -->
> **Last updated Oct 30, 2026** · **821** internships · **310** new-grad roles · **49** companies · US only

**Jump to:** [Internships](#internships) · [New Grad](#new-grad) · [About this list](#about) · 🆕 = added in the last 7 days

<a id="about"></a>

## About this list

The list covers chip design, verification, physical design, DFT, process, device, test, firmware and related roles. Jobs come straight from company career sites and chip-industry job boards, filtered to internships, co-ops and new-grad / entry-level roles in the US. It's refreshed every morning.

Always confirm the details on the company's own posting before applying.

### Where the jobs come from

**Company career sites:** most large chip companies post university roles on their own sites first.

| Type | Companies |
| --- | --- |
| Chipmakers | AMD · Analog Devices · Arm · Broadcom · Cirrus Logic · Infineon · Intel · Kioxia · Lattice · Marvell · Microchip · Micron · Monolithic Power · NVIDIA · NXP · onsemi · Qorvo · Qualcomm · Renesas · Samsung Semiconductor · Semtech · Silicon Labs · SK hynix America · Skyworks · STMicroelectronics · Texas Instruments · Vishay · Western Digital · Wolfspeed · Allegro Microsystems |
| Foundry, equipment & materials | Applied Materials · Coherent · Entegris · FormFactor · GlobalFoundries · KLA · Lam Research · MKS Instruments · Onto Innovation · Teradyne |
| EDA & IP | Cadence · Synopsys · SiFive |
| Startups | Ambiq · Astera Labs · Axelera AI · Cerebras · d-Matrix · Eliyan · Etched · Extropic · Groq · Lightmatter · MatX · Normal Computing · PsiQuantum · SambaNova · Tenstorrent |

**Job boards**

- [SemiDesignJobs](https://www.semidesignjobs.com): chip-design roles from many more companies.
- [Semiconductor Engineering jobs](https://semiengineering.com/jobs/): the board attached to the main industry trade publication.

Each source's connection details are in [`sources.json`](sources.json).

### Check these by hand too

These can't be scraped because they need a login or have no public data:

| Source | Why it's worth checking |
| --- | --- |
| [ChipPath](https://semifoundation.org/chippath/) (SEMI Foundation) | The largest US semiconductor careers hub (~90k openings), plus resume and interview tools. Sign up through FutureFit AI. |
| [SIA Jobs](https://www.semiconductors.org/sia-jobs/) | The Semiconductor Industry Association's board. |
| [Handshake](https://joinhandshake.com) | Where companies recruiting at your school post. |
| [LinkedIn Jobs](https://www.linkedin.com/jobs/) | Covers everything else. Set an alert for "semiconductor intern". |
| [EDGE Consortium](https://www.edgeconsortium.org/) and [Olin EDGE](https://app.notion.com/p/EDGE-Consortium-Olin-40006a00232f80b69684dc0de1c93965) events | Talent-acquisition webinars and site visits are a hiring pipeline, so treat them as such. |
| Not scraped yet | Their career sites block scrapers or have no public data: TSMC Arizona, ASML, Sandisk, Rambus, MACOM, Amkor, Ampere, Synaptics, Power Integrations, Ayar Labs. |

<details>
<summary><b>How it works</b></summary>

1. `scrape.py` downloads every open posting from each source in [`sources.json`](sources.json). That's about 25,000 postings, and a run takes about 5 minutes.
2. It keeps titles that look like internships (intern, co-op, student, summer 2027…) or new-grad roles (new college grad, university, entry level, Engineer I…). It drops senior, staff, manager and similar titles. Many career sites also label roles "Intern" or "New College Graduate" directly, and the scraper uses those labels too.
3. It keeps US locations. Roles whose location is unclear ("Remote", "3 Locations") are kept as well.
4. It merges duplicates when the same job shows up on a company site and a job board, keeping the company link.
5. It writes:
   - the tables in this README (between the `JOBS:START` / `JOBS:END` markers)
   - [`site/jobs.json`](site/jobs.json) for the website
   - [`data/jobs.csv`](data/jobs.csv) for spreadsheets
6. Jobs first seen in the last 7 days get a 🆕 badge. If a source fails, its jobs from the previous run are kept rather than disappearing.

[`.github/workflows/update.yml`](.github/workflows/update.yml) runs this every morning, commits the new list, and publishes the website.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Run it yourself</b></summary>

Requires Python 3.9+.

```bash
pip install -r requirements.txt
python scrape.py                              # US roles from all sources
python scrape.py --all-locations              # include roles outside the US
python scrape.py --only NVIDIA,Intel --dry-run   # test a few sources without writing files
```

To preview the website locally, run `python -m http.server -d site 8000` and open http://localhost:8000.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Add a company</b></summary>

Add an entry to [`sources.json`](sources.json), above the job boards (company links take priority over a board's copy of the same job). Then check it with `python scrape.py --only "Company Name" --dry-run`.

| Career site | How to spot it | Entry |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Workday | Job URLs look like `https://{tenant}.wd5.myworkdayjobs.com/{site}/job/...` | `{"company": "X", "ats": "workday", "host": "x.wd5.myworkdayjobs.com", "tenant": "x", "site": "External"}` |
| Greenhouse | `boards.greenhouse.io/{token}` | `{"company": "X", "ats": "greenhouse", "token": "x"}` |
| Lever | `jobs.lever.co/{token}` | `{"company": "X", "ats": "lever", "token": "x"}` |
| Ashby | `jobs.ashbyhq.com/{token}` | `{"company": "X", "ats": "ashby", "token": "x"}` |
| SmartRecruiters | `jobs.smartrecruiters.com/{token}` | `{"company": "X", "ats": "smartrecruiters", "token": "X"}` |
| Eightfold | `/careers/job/123…` URLs on the company domain | `{"company": "X", "ats": "eightfold", "host": "careers.x.com", "domain": "x.com"}` |
| Oracle HCM | `*.oraclecloud.com/hcmUI/CandidateExperience/.../sites/{site}` | `{"company": "X", "ats": "oracle", "host": "abcd.fa.us2.oraclecloud.com", "site": "CX"}` |
| SuccessFactors | The site serves `/sitemal.xml` | `{"company": "X", "ats": "successfactors_rss", "host": "careers.x.com"}` |
| Radancy / TalentBrew | Uses `/search-jobs` pages | `{"company": "X", "ats": "radancy", "host": "careers.x.com"}` |

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Set up the daily update and website</b> (one time, for the repo owner)</summary>

1. Push this repo to GitHub.
2. **Settings → Pages → Build and deployment → Source:** choose **GitHub Actions**.
3. **Actions tab:** enable workflows, then open **Update jobs** and click **Run workflow** once.

After that it runs every morning at 8am Eastern. It commits the updated README / data, and the website is live at `https://<user>.github.io/college-semiconductor-jobs/`.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Optional: GitHub sign-in for the tracker</b></summary>

Out of the box, **Sync with GitHub** asks visitors to paste a personal access token with the `gist` scope. Their tracker is then saved to a private gist in their own account, and nothing goes to this repo or to anyone else.

For a one-click **Sign in with GitHub** button instead:

1. Create an OAuth App at **GitHub → Settings → Developer settings → OAuth Apps**. Set both the Homepage URL and the Authorization callback URL to your site URL (e.g. `https://rcyaon.github.io/college-semiconductor-jobs/`).
2. Deploy [`worker/github-oauth.js`](worker/github-oauth.js) as a free Cloudflare Worker. Give it these settings:
   - `GITHUB_CLIENT_ID` (variable)
   - `GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET` (secret)
   - `ALLOWED_ORIGIN`, e.g. `https://rcyaon.github.io`
3. Put the Client ID and the worker URL into [`site/config.js`](site/config.js).

The worker is needed because GitHub's sign-in requires a client secret, which a static site can't keep.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Project layout</b></summary>

```
scrape.py              entry point: fetch → filter → merge → write
sources.json           companies and job boards to scrape
semijobs/sources.py    one fetcher per career-site type / job board
semijobs/filters.py    intern / new-grad / US-location rules
semijobs/report.py     writes the README tables, site/jobs.json, data/jobs.csv
site/                  the website (GitHub Pages): index.html, app.js, style.css, config.js
worker/                optional GitHub sign-in helper (Cloudflare Worker)
data/                  jobs.csv, seen.json (first-seen dates for 🆕)
.github/workflows/     daily update + website deploy
```

</details>
